Statement: Let the biomedical_engineering structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 166. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 399 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_166(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 166. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^399(S, F) is trivial. QED.
